### WP23: Credit flows and return exceptions: advance payments, cash refunds, return override, undelivered credit note

**الحالة (2026-10-31): done (WP23)** — merged into the integration branch `int/w4` under E47 ([E85](../decisions.md), [E86](../decisions.md), [report](wp23-report.md), branch head `1e564c1`): the customer credit order of E57 (invoice date, then the earlier-created invoice by id, no origin-first; the supplier order stays origin-first), the customer advance payment as a credit on the customer account, the cash refund of customer and supplier credit with the credit it was taken from, the return override with its permission and marker, and the credit note on an undelivered invoice with no stock and no cost journal. 144 methods in seven new files (counted from the report) and changes in 25 existing test files (the branch diff adds 259 `public function test` lines and removes 10), 102 guards each disabled alone and detected (102 of 102; `wp23-guards.json` holds 102; not re-run by the integrator), fifteen review claims all upheld in whole or in part. One migration, `20271200000000_CreditFlows` (six tables and four permissions granted to no one; additive in the readiness command). **Not done:** the advance-payment model and the other owner decisions (WP23-OWNER-DECISIONS, WP23-E57-SUPPLIER-ORDER, WP23-REFUND-NUMBERING), what Daftra was not observed doing (G-156), the lists, the advance tax, the race test and the label of the purchase form (G-157), the untested combination with the opening balance and the cheque credit (G-163), the browser pass (G-165).

**لماذا هنا:** A customer cannot pay without an invoice, credit cannot be refunded in cash, a stock return without an origin is only refused and an undelivered invoice can only be corrected by confirming delivery first. High accounting risk (customer and supplier liabilities), so it waits for the observations of WP22 (F-10, F-11, F-12).

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Triple tie: customer (supplier) balance equals aging equals account balance after every operation
- Advance payment follows the observed shape (E74, batch C, per the observer's report with no evidence file kept): Dr treasury and the prepaid tax (1213), Cr a per-customer advance-payments liability (218xxx) and the output tax (215001), the customer account untouched until a manual, capped distribution; the distribution reverses and reposts the invoice journal (E15, E62) instead of rewriting it in place; idempotent, reversed not deleted. This replaces the earlier assumption (Dr treasury, Cr customer)
- Cash refund respects treasury withdraw scope, branch, period locks and posts Dr customer (supplier) Cr treasury
- Return override needs a permission, an audit flag and a journal marker; ReturnLimit stays enforced; credit note on an undelivered invoice moves no stock and posts no cost journal
- Credit applications stay journal-less and unwind explicitly (E25, E30)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- CreditSettlementTest cases for advance payments and the observed application order (date versus number)
- SalePostingTest: override flag, audit entry, cost at current cost; credit note on undelivered invoice has no stock or cost journal
- Refund journal ties to TreasuryService::position; closed period; branch scope; reversal
- Legacy returns without origin unaffected; SupplierSettlementTest for the supplier refund

**رصد دفترة (2026-10-28):** **Observation requirement met except the tie-break (2026-10-28):** D1 observed F-10, F-11, F-12 and F-13 (E57, E63); the cash-refund path and the advance payment need batch C.
- قرار مطلوب (تقارير المبيعات والعملاء): أُكِّد ترتيب التطبيق بكتابة مضبوطة (D1، E57): لا أفضلية للفاتورة الأصل ويبقى كسر التعادل غير مرصود؛ تغيير فرع «الأصل أولًا» في `CreditSettlement::apply` هو البند `E57-CREDIT-ORDER` ويعيد تصنيف اختبار الأصل في `CreditSettlementTest`.
- **قواعد مرصودة** (1؛ النص الكامل وأدلتها في [synthesis.md](observations/obs-a/synthesis.md)): `RSC-CRD-01`
- **الدفعة C (2026-10-31، [ملاحظات C](observations/c/NOTES.md)، بحسب تقرير الراصد: لا ملف دليل محفوظ):** the tie-break (E57) and the advance payment (E74) are observed, which closes the observation requirement of this package except the cash refund of an advance, a distribution over several invoices and cancelling a distributed one; the advance-payments report came back empty for the one advance created (unexplained).

### WP24: Routing types per-item and main-account-per-entity

**الحالة (2026-11-07): done (WP24)** — merged into the integration branch `int/w6` under E47 ([E110](../decisions.md), [report](wp24-report.md), branch head `6d01071`), on dev data only: type 2 (per item) works on the sales revenue and returns rows (an account per item in the new table `item_routing_accounts`, the account of the row when the item names none, and a named stop when an item names an account that no longer takes entries) and type 4 (main account per entity) on the customers row; a reversal copies the lines of the original and never re-resolves the routing; automatic-routing journals are byte-identical (the golden `T-06` to `T-19`); three readiness checks (`WP24-ROUTING-TYPES`, `WP24-ITEM-ROUTING`, `WP24-ENTITY-ROUTING`). One additive migration, `20272100000000_ItemRoutingAccounts`. 165 `public function test` lines added in the branch diff (3 removed), 67 guards (`wp24-guards.json` holds 67; each disabled alone and detected per the report; not re-run by the integrator). **Not done:** type 2 on the stock and cost-of-sales rows and a default per category (G-195, an owner decision: it needs the three stock ties rebuilt on account and location), the choice of a parent account per customer, S-28 and S-29 unobserved (G-196), and the browser pass of the item form (G-216). **Found by reading the merged code:** a foreign-currency invoice whose items all name accounts of their own cannot find the line that carries the rounding difference (G-197). The meaning of the two types is an assumption, not a Daftra match.

**لماذا هنا:** The settings exist (P2) but posting refuses the two types (RoutingResolver throws RoutingTypeUnsupported). Needs the Daftra previews S-28 and S-29 to know which line they replace.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Documents with default (auto/cancelled) routing post byte-identical journals (golden tests over T-06 to T-19)
- The per-item account is an active postable leaf; a reversal mirrors the original journal's accounts and never re-resolves routing
- Historical documents keep the accounts they were posted with; settings changes are audited
- Readiness checker knows the new types

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- RoutingResolverTest/RoutingKeyTest/SalePostingBuilderTest with item and entity overrides
- Reverse a sale posted under per-item routing after the setting changed: reversal mirrors
- Legacy documents and the readiness codes

**الحالة (2026-10-31): done (WP26)** — merged into the integration branch `int/w4` under E47 ([E87](../decisions.md), [E88](../decisions.md), [E89](../decisions.md), [report](wp26-report.md), branch head `d9c678f`): the cheque cycle for received and paid cheques with a journal per transition and nine journal sources, the cheque books, the cheque credit that settles open invoices, the cheque list and its two reports, the receipt voucher and the revenue categories, and the proof that the payment method is independent of the treasury. 359 test methods added (310 in 14 new files, 49 in existing files; the branch diff counts 359 as well), 398 guards each disabled alone and detected (per the report; `wp26-guards.json` holds 398; not re-run by the integrator), two independent reviews (19 notes fixed, 3 refused with a recorded reason). ### WP27: Stock documents: numbered stock permits, stocktake document, stock requests

**الحالة (2026-11-07): done (WP27)** — merged into `int/w6` under E47 ([E111](../decisions.md), [report](wp27-report.md), branch head `2f041e1`), on dev data only: numbered stock permits (add, issue, transfer between two locations, six digits) posted through the existing builders, cancelled by reversal and edited by reversal and re-post; a stocktake with a frozen system quantity and one journal at the date of approval against 511 (approval is a permission of its own and carries a digest of the lines reviewed; one stocktake per location and day; an item on one open stocktake of its location); stock requests that post nothing and whose fulfilment creates the transfer permit; the transfer permit and the requests are behind two settings, off by default. - Transfers through requisition receivings stay valid until a permit wraps them; valued movements and journals are unchanged
- Documents are numbered (WP8), carry a count date and use the stock_permits routing accounts
- StockLedger::reconcile stays at zero; branch scope and closed periods enforced; posted opening stock untouched

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- ReceivingPostingTest regressions; StockPermitTest and StocktakeTest (new) tie to StockLedger::reconcile
- Branch scope and closed-period refusal
- Legacy StockAdjustment journals remain valid

### WP28: Multi-currency: document currency and rate, FX difference entries, treasury currency, non-two-decimal currencies

**الحالة (2026-11-07): done (WP28), partly** — merged into `int/w6` under E47 ([E112](../decisions.md), [report](wp28-report.md), branch head `670a009`), on dev data only: the currencies and rates tables with the sensitive permission `acc_currencies_manage`, the document currency of sales, purchases, returns and payments posted in the base with the foreign amount on the line, one exchange-difference journal per payment (`sale_payment_fx` and `supplier_payment_fx`, to `fx.gain` 4209 and `fx.loss` 5410) reversed with its payment, the treasury currency and a transfer between two currencies by both amounts, and the refusal by name of what is not supported. Three migrations: `20272300000000_CurrenciesAndRates` (additive), `20272300000001_DocumentCurrencyColumns` (HEAVY, nine tables) and `20272300000002_FxDifferenceAccounts` (DATA). 328 `public function test` lines added in the branch diff (none removed), 183 guards (`wp28-guards.json` holds 183; the 24 review claims were fixed with a test each). **Not done (reduced on purpose):** the foreign expense, voucher, cheque and advance (G-205), the revaluation at period end (G-206), three decimals on documents (G-207), the report filter by currency, the legacy reports, the printed receipt and the purchases register screen (G-208), the Daftra points (G-209) and the owner's choices (WP28-OWNER-DECISIONS). **Found by reading the merged code:** the sales, purchase and supplier-payment reports of WP32 and WP33 do not know the document currency (G-202). Multi-currency stays off in production until the owner decides.

- Base-currency journals unchanged (currency_code base, rate 1); foreign_amount optional

- Per-currency decimals (0, 2, 3) honoured by rounding
- Historical journals untouched; rate captured at document time; reports in base currency

- Payment at a different rate posts the FX difference (S-30 fixture)

### WP29: Banking: bank reconciliation and bank directory

**الحالة (2026-11-01): done (WP29)** — merged into the integration branch `int/w5` under E47 ([E97](../decisions.md), [E98](../decisions.md), [E99](../decisions.md), [E100](../decisions.md), [report](wp29-report.md), branch head `3315463`), on dev data only: a fixed directory of 56 banks chosen in the bank-account form, a CSV import of a bank statement (full check, fingerprint, once only), a manual match of a statement line to one or more journal lines of the account, read-only suggestions, a reconciliation report that ties to the treasury position and the ledger, and a closing until a date with a reopening. Nothing writes a journal. One HEAVY migration, `20271700000000_BankReconciliation` (a nullable column and five new tables). The permission `acc_bank_reconcile` is not sensitive (an explicit decision of the report) and is granted to no one. 228 test lines added in the branch diff (none removed), 221 guards (`wp29-guards.json` holds 221; detected per the report; not re-run). **Not done:** a voucher from a statement line (G-173), Excel and Windows-1256, the running balance, an export and auto-matching (G-174), the Daftra match itself (G-175), the owner questions (WP29-OWNER-DECISIONS) and the browser pass (G-194).

**لماذا هنا:** Bank balances tie to the ledger but nothing matches statement lines; the bank name is free text. E47 prohibits paid external integrations without separate permission, so this stays manual or static.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Reconciliation never edits a journal; matched lines are flagged
- Statement import is checksummed and idempotent
- No paid external service without separate permission (E47)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- BankReconciliationTest: match, unmatch, idempotent import

### WP30: Roles and own-versus-all record scope

**الحالة (2026-11-01): done (WP30)** — merged into the integration branch `int/w5` under E47 ([E101](../decisions.md), [report](wp30-report.md), branch head `c491360`), on dev data only: the role as an entity (name, description, active, scope) with permissions of the same ids as the grants and the effective permission as the union of the employee's grants and the grants of the roles (`Employee::has_grant`), no sensitive permission and no branch scope in a role, the own-records option for a role and for an employee enforced in the models and services, a default warehouse per employee, and the treasury scope 'roles' made valid. Two additive migrations (`20271800000000_RolesAndRecordScope`, five new tables, and `20271800000001_RolesPermissions`). 267 test lines added in the branch diff (one removed or renamed), 167 guards (`wp30-guards.json` holds 167; detected per the report; not re-run). **Not done:** receipt vouchers, cheques, treasury transfers, stock documents, the reports and the customer and supplier statements are outside the own scope (G-176), subsumption, per-document view and edit options, the catalog mapping and the seeded roles (G-177), the employee lifecycle (G-123), the Daftra points (G-178), the owner questions (WP30-OWNER-DECISIONS) and the browser pass (G-194).

**لماذا هنا:** The app has per-employee module grants and an own/all switch only for manual journals; Daftra has roles with 130+ options scoped all or own. XL and cross-cutting, so it follows the control-gap fixes and the observation of the role matrix and a restricted employee (S-32). Also carries the default warehouse per employee.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Existing per-employee grants keep working; roles are additive and existing employees default to scope 'all'
- Sensitive permissions stay administrator-only; branch scope unchanged
- Own-scope is enforced in services, not only screens
- The treasury 'roles' scope (rejected today in TreasuryScopesTest) becomes valid only when roles exist

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- EmployeesControllerTest and per-document own/all tests for sales, receivings and journals (acc_journals_all precedent)
- TreasuryScopesTest updated for the roles mode
- Default warehouse per employee; denial messages after S-32

**رصد دفترة (2026-10-28):** **Observation requirement met except denial messages (2026-10-28):** OBS-A read the role matrix, its dependency relation and the own-versus-all scope; S-32 needs a second user (batch C).

### WP31: Report parity: Daftra filters and layouts, third trial-balance type, statement print header, cash-basis profit and loss, cash flow

**الحالة (2026-10-31): done (WP31)** — merged into the integration branch `int/w4` under E47 ([E90](../decisions.md), [report](wp31-report.md), [design summary](a8-design.md), branch head `27c627a`): the fourth trial-balance type under the name 'totals before, movement and after' (not 'review account'), the trial-balance filters, the ledger with the employee filter and a block per account, profit and loss on the accrual basis by month, quarter and year and on the cash basis from the movement of the treasuries and banks, the indirect cash flow with a configurable set of lines, and CSV files for all of them (read-only except the saved lines). 263 test methods added in 15 files plus the migration test (the branch diff adds 263 `public function test` lines), 105 guards each disabled alone and detected (105 of 105; `wp31-guards.json` holds 105; not re-run by the integrator). One migration, `20271400000000_AppConfigValueText`, which widens `app_config.value` to `TEXT`: **a core OSPOS table, and the readiness command prints it as additive (G-162)**. **Not done:** the print header and the PDF and Excel contents (R-05), the two refused bases of the cash profit (REP-CASHBASIS-INVOICE), the monthly columns of the cash profit and the two-period comparison (G-84), the unobserved Daftra points (G-161), the owner decisions (WP31-OWNER-DECISIONS, G-97, G-100, P8-TB3), and the browser pass (G-165).

**لماذا هنا:** Trial balance and ledger shipped but cannot be called matching until identical inputs are compared; cash-basis recognition, the cash-flow layout, the third trial-balance type and the print header are unobserved. Needs the read-only evidence of WP20.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Read-only; branch rules of E45; totals tie to the ledger
- Honest naming: the third shape keeps its own name until it matches Daftra's
- Parity fixtures record Daftra numbers only (122,478 = 122,478)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- Fixtures T-26 onward (T-25 is the WP3 expense journal) for trial balance and ledger on the A/B scenario documents
- TrialBalanceTest and AccountLedgerTest ties; print header and export controller tests
- a8-design.md written before UI code (phase checklist)

### WP32: Operational report families: sales, items, payments, profit, net position

**الحالة (2026-11-07): done (WP32), partly** — merged into `int/w6` under E47 ([E113](../decisions.md), [report](wp32-report.md), branch head `bc91955`), on dev data only: nine read-only reports under `/accounting_reports` from the posted ledger (`revenue`, `clients_sales`, `item_sales`, `profit`, `item_profit`, `payments`, `advance_payments`, `net_position` and `invoice_returns`), each with a tie table against the books, one distribution rule for paid, unpaid and returned, a profit that equals the cost-of-sales journals, and a page that shows nothing until it is run. No migration, no module and no permission (the existing `reports_*` ones by dimension). 234 `public function test` lines added in the branch diff (none removed), 167 guards (`wp32-guards.json` holds 167, with a `category` on each; the review claims were fixed with a test and a guard each). **Not done (reduced on purpose):** the salesperson and the brand, PDF and Excel, the supplier reports and the balance filters (G-210), the Daftra points (G-211) and the owner's questions (WP32-OWNER-DECISIONS); the legacy `Summary_*` reports are unchanged. **Found by reading the merged code:** the reports do not know the document currency (G-202) and do not apply the own-records scope that the reports of WP33 apply (G-203).

**لماذا هنا:** Only date-range summaries exist; Daftra has daily, weekly, monthly and yearly variants by customer, user, representative, brand, method and profit from valued movements. Columns were only catalogued, not read.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Read-only; branch scope from WP1
- Profit derives from valued movements and agrees with account 511
- Operational reports stay unchanged until replaced

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- ReportsControllerTest per variant
- Profit total equals 511 for the period; payment report equals the treasury journals
- Branch scope and permissions

**رصد دفترة (2026-10-28):** **Observation requirement met (2026-10-28):** OBS-A read 14 revenue views, 20 item views, 9 payment views, two profit reports and the net-position reports.

### WP33: Report families: purchases and suppliers, inventory aging and turnover, expenses and income splits, journal, chart and cost-centre reports, activity log

**الحالة (2026-11-07): done (WP33)** — merged into `int/w6` under E47 ([E114](../decisions.md), [report](wp33-report.md), branch head `0d29fd8`), on dev data only: twenty-one read-only reports in one catalog with one table engine and one CSV (purchases, suppliers, stock, expenses, income, journals, chart and financial movements), one definition of net purchases, each with a branch scope and a tie row, the treasury statement with real paging, the transfer filter, and the activity log with paging, a source filter, preset periods and a file (limit 20,000). No migration, no module and no permission. 217 `public function test` lines added in the branch diff (none removed), 105 guards (`wp33-guards.json` holds 105). **Not done:** the business events of the activity log (owner decision), the overdue state of purchases and the cost-centre columns (after WP38), brand grouping, PDF and Excel (G-212), and the Daftra points (G-213). **Found by reading the merged code:** the stock operations summary and the journal source filter lack the sources of WP27 and WP28 (G-200, G-201), the reports do not know the document currency (G-202), and two parallel table engines exist (G-204).

**لماذا هنا:** Purchase, inventory, expense and journal reports are partial; layouts were never read. REP-14 covers what WP18 leaves (count sheet, movements, warehouse and product balance, aging, turnover).

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Read-only; branch scope; supplier balances tie to SupplierLedger and 2211
- Inventory reports tie to 1203 per location
- Expense and income splits tie to the expense journals (WP3)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- ReportsControllerTest per report
- Ties to SupplierLedger, StockLedger::reconcile and expense journals
- Activity log filters and permissions

**رصد دفترة (2026-10-28):** **Observation requirement met except the cost-centre and receipt-voucher columns (2026-10-28):** OBS-A read the purchase, supplier, inventory, expense and activity-log reports and the journal and chart reports.

### WP34: Invoice header charges, sales debit note, journal description template

**الحالة (2026-11-01): done (WP34)** — merged into the integration branch `int/w5` under E47 ([E102](../decisions.md), [E103](../decisions.md), [E104](../decisions.md), [report](wp34-report.md), branch head `e87aec9`), on dev data only: the adjustment and the shipping charge on the invoice, the quote, the credit note and the refund invoice, posted through the routing rows `sales.adjustment` (`4204`) and `sales.shipping` (`4208`) with the revenue as the balancing figure; the sales debit note as a document with a journal, a five-digit number, an open item in aging and in the E57 order; and the journal description template. All off by default. Two migrations, `20271900000000_InvoiceHeaderCharges` (DATA: it opens two accounts in an existing chart) and `20271900000001_SalesDebitNotes` (HEAVY: it rebuilds `customer_credit_applications` a second time). 296 test lines added in the branch diff (four removed or renamed), 183 guards (`wp34-guards.json` holds 183; detected per the report; not re-run). **Not done:** the operational sales reports without the charges (G-181), the debit note scope, edit, print and parent invoice (G-182), a shipping tax chosen per document and the purchase charges (G-183), the template for other journals (G-184), the permission of the charges that give money and the bound of the credit note (G-185), the unobserved journals (G-186), the owner questions (WP34-OWNER-DECISIONS) and the browser pass (G-194). **`acc_debit_notes_manage` is not sensitive (G-179).**

**لماذا هنا:** Adjustment, shipping, sales debit note and templated journal descriptions have no counterpart and their journal and tax treatment was never observed.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Adjustment and shipping post through routing rows (SalesAdjustment is refused today by assertUnusedRows)
- New nullable columns only; posted invoices unchanged; E14 rounding
- Debit note aging and journal effect follow the observation

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- SalePostingBuilderTest with adjustment and shipping fixtures
- Debit note journals tie to the customer ledger; description template tests
- Legacy descriptions unchanged

**رصد دفترة (2026-10-28):** OBS-A read the header charges (adjustment, shipping, deposit); the sales debit note needs its setting enabled (batch C).
- يُضاف (النماذج): Header charges observed: adjustment (label text + signed number, added only while the running total > 0, gated by enable_sales_adjustment which is on, present on invoice and credit note, absent on purchases where the setting is off), shipping (options «آلي» /…
- اعتماد جديد (النماذج): The sales debit note (SAL-20) has only its setting (enable_debit_note off, next 00001, numbering heading «الاشعارات المدينة للمبيعات») observed; its form and journal need the setting enabled in the trial account and reverted (OBS-C then OBS-D).
- اعتماد جديد (الأدوار): الإشعار المدين للمبيعات يحمل في دفترة ستة خيارات صلاحية (427 إلى 432: إضافة وتعديل وحذف وعرض بنطاق الكل والخاص) ونوع ترقيم 34 بخمس منازل (5 منازل افتراضيًا، التالي 00001)؛ يُسجَّلان وقت بناء المستند.
- **قواعد مرصودة** (4؛ النص الكامل وأدلتها في [synthesis.md](observations/obs-a/synthesis.md)): `FRM-R04`، `FRM-R06`، `FRM-R07`، `FRM-R08`

### WP35: Order-to-cash documents: sales orders, recurring invoices, recurring expenses

**الحالة (2026-11-07): done (WP35)** — merged into `int/w6` under E47 ([E115 to E120](../decisions.md), [report](wp35-report.md), branch head `4b27013`), on dev data only: sales orders (module `customer_orders`, five-digit numbering, stored and read states, a conversion that fills the register, quantities taken at the save of the completed invoice under the lock of the order), recurring invoices and expenses (dates counted from the start, the generator `accounting:generate_recurring` with one unit of work per date and a unique key, named refusals with a manual retry, in-app reminders only), and the readiness check `WP35-RECURRING` (the 25th). One additive migration, `20272600000000_OrderToCashDocuments` (eight tables, three modules and their permissions granted to no one). The command is run from the cron of the owner and installed by no code. 335 `public function test` lines added in the branch diff (1 removed), 223 guards (`wp35-guards.json` holds 223). **Built on assumptions** where Daftra was not observed (the states of an order, the generation timing): no match is claimed (G-215). **Not done:** auto-pay, send-me-a-copy, recurring income, printing and the reminder mail (G-214); `WP14-DRAFT-COPY-PAYMENT` and `WP14-QUOTE-VALIDITY` stay open.

**لماذا هنا:** No sales order, subscription or expense recurrence exists; Daftra's generation timing and document behaviour were deliberately not observed (S-34 creates future documents).

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Generated documents follow the normal posting path and numbering
- Generation is idempotent per schedule and date
- No posting for orders until invoiced

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- Generator command test with repeated runs and a closed period
- Conversion tests; branch scope

**رصد دفترة (2026-10-28):** OBS-A read the recurring invoice and expense forms; sales orders need their setting enabled (batch C).
- يُضاف (النماذج): Recurring invoice (add_subscription) and recurring expense forms are observed: schedule fields, validation (issue-before days must be less than period x 1/7/30/365 for days/weeks/months/years; period and max repeat >= 0; name required), up to 10 reminders…

- **قواعد مرصودة** (5؛ النص الكامل وأدلتها في [synthesis.md](observations/obs-a/synthesis.md)): `FRM-R14` إلى `FRM-R18`
- **الدفعة C (2026-10-31، [ملاحظات C](observations/c/NOTES.md)، بحسب تقرير الراصد: لا ملف دليل محفوظ):** the sales-order setting, the form and one draft were observed; the statuses and the conversion are not (`G-149`).

### WP36: Pricing and credit controls: price lists, minimum price, maximum discount, excluded categories, credit limit, preview and profit tab

**الحالة (2026-11-01): done (WP36)** — merged into the integration branch `int/w5` under E47 ([E105](../decisions.md), [E106](../decisions.md), [E107](../decisions.md), [report](wp36-report.md), branch head `6d8dd86`), on dev data only: price lists (name, status, a price per item entered on the item form) that set the line price and are copied to it, a minimum price and a maximum discount (per item or for the shop) with excluded categories, checked at the register and again at issue inside the save transaction, and a credit limit and period checked at issue under the customer lock; all off by default, with two sensitive override permissions granted to no one (`sales_price_override`, `sales_credit_override`). One HEAVY migration, `20272000000000_PricingAndCreditControls`. 286 test lines added in the branch diff (none removed), 135 guards (`wp36-guards.json` holds 135; detected per the report; not re-run). **Not done:** the preview and the profit tab, the last sale price, selling under average cost and the include-tax minimum (G-188), the discount per employee and the sale ban by category (G-189), the credit limit per branch and the draft check (G-190), price lists in purchases and the bulk update (G-191), the Daftra points (G-192), **the minimum price does not know the tax (G-193) and the credit limit has no test with debit notes and opening balances (G-187), both found by reading the merged code**, the owner questions (WP36-OWNER-DECISIONS) and the browser pass (G-194).

**لماذا هنا:** Only the settings were seen in Daftra. Credit limit is a financial control (needs open amounts from the customer ledger and, for the period, due dates from WP13).

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Credit-limit check uses CustomerLedger open amounts inside the save transaction
- Price controls never change posted documents
- Permission to override is explicit

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- SalesControllerTest for each control
- Credit-limit race test (two real connections)
- Item model tests for minimum price, discount and margin

**رصد دفترة (2026-10-28):** OBS-A read the price-list form and the minimum-price check; credit limit and maximum discount need their settings enabled (batch C).
- يُضاف (تقارير المبيعات والعملاء): إعدادات كشف الحساب في دفترة تحمل متغيرين يحتاجان حقلين عندنا: «الحد الائتماني» و«فترة السماح» (يظهران في مقدمة الكشف المخصصة). فيدخلان قائمة حقول العميل مع CUS-04 ويُعرضان في مقدمة الكشف عند بنائها. لا أثر على قفل الحد الائتماني نفسه.
- يُضاف (النماذج): Price list create form: «الاسم *» and «الحالة *» (نشط default / غير نشط) only; per-list prices live on the product form (placeholder «use default price»); default list and change-on-invoice settings (off). Minimum price: product field «أقل سعر بيع» with a…
- اعتماد جديد (النماذج): Credit limit and credit period need their two settings enabled in the trial account and reverted to observe the blocking message and what counts as outstanding (OBS-C then OBS-D); the same for the maximum-discount setting and the excluded-categories lists.
- **قواعد مرصودة** (2؛ النص الكامل وأدلتها في [synthesis.md](observations/obs-a/synthesis.md)): `FRM-R19`، `FRM-R20`
- **الدفعة C (2026-10-31، [ملاحظات C](observations/c/NOTES.md)، بحسب تقرير الراصد: لا ملف دليل محفوظ):** the credit-limit rule (E77) and the price list that sets the line price (E78) are observed; the minimum-price refusal at issue, the maximum discount per user and the excluded categories are not (`G-149`).

### WP41: Integration settings: payment gateways and methods, secondary language, auto-send rules, files and API keys

**الحالة (2026-11-13): done (WP41), partly** — merged into `int/w8` under E47 ([E143](../decisions.md), [report](wp41-report.md), branch head `cae73c6`), on dev data only: manual payment methods that can be stopped and renamed for display (a stopped method refuses a new payment and the old record keeps it), the second language of customer, supplier, item and category names printed under the first in the sales documents and the purchase invoice, read-only API keys under `/api/v1` with scopes, a branch limit and a secret shown once, and in-app notification rules; **everything is off by default**, nothing leaves the shop (no gateway, mail, SMS or outgoing webhook: E47). One migration (seven new tables, two modules, four permissions granted to no one, `api_keys_manage` sensitive). 302 `public function test` lines added (none removed) and 233 mutations (`wp41-guards.json`, all detected). Not built: payment gateways, automatic mail and SMS, an outgoing webhook, the files library, app activation per account (a recorded difference, G-130), second-language printing in the other documents and write routes in the API (G-253).

**لماذا هنا:** Manual payment types with routing exist; gateways, entity second language, rules engine and API keys do not. E47 forbids paid external integrations without permission.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Gateway payments post only on completed status through the existing payment journal path
- API keys are scoped and never logged
- No paid external integration without separate permission

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- Payment-status tests for gateway results
- Rules-engine tests
- API authentication tests

**رصد دفترة (2026-10-28):** OBS-A read the role options and the apps manager; the secrets screens need an owner decision on their permission.
- قرار مطلوب (الأدوار): لا خيار في مصفوفة الدور لطرق الدفع ولا SMTP ولا API ولا إدارة التطبيقات ولا الترقيم ولا قواعد الإرسال الآلي (استنتاج: للمالك فقط). القرار: تُحجب هذه الشاشات عندنا بصلاحية `config` الموجودة أم بمنحة حساسة جديدة للأسرار؟ التوصية: مفاتيح API وكلمة SMTP حساسة…
- قرار مطلوب (الطباعة وإقفال السنة): هل نحتاج مفهوم تفعيل التطبيقات لكل حساب (36 تطبيقًا في دفترة، 7 مفعّلة)؟ البديل القائم وحدات بصلاحيات الموظف ومفتاح محاسبة D1. التوصية: لا تفعيل لكل حساب الآن، وتسجيل الفرق قرارًا، ويُعاد النظر حين تُبنى وحدة اختيارية مستقلة (الشيكات أو الفروع).
- **قواعد مرصودة** (1؛ النص الكامل وأدلتها في [synthesis.md](observations/obs-a/synthesis.md)): `RP-SEC-02`

### WP42: Multiple units, bundles and batch, serial and expiry tracking

**الحالة (2026-11-08): done (WP42), partly** — merged into `int/w7` under E47 ([E132](../decisions.md), [report](wp42-report.md), branch head `3de7c36`), on dev data only and **on assumptions where Daftra was not observed (G-232)**: unit templates and lines in a larger unit stored in the base unit with an exact quantity and price, bundles on the existing kits, tracking by serial number and by batch with expiry (the sum of the batches or of the available serials equals the quantity of the location, always), and the three negative-stock options, all behind settings that are off (negative stock `allow`), with the readiness checks `WP42-TRACKING` and `WP42-BUNDLES`. One migration, `20273100000000_StockUnitsAndTracking` (HEAVY: nullable unit columns and a shape check on three line tables, and eight new tables; the module `stock_units` and three permissions granted to no one). 324 `public function test` lines added and 193 guards. Not built (G-229): the pre-assembled bundle, a price and a cost per batch, tracking of supplier returns, requisition transfers, receiving corrections and the stocktake for a tracked item (refused by name), and moving an existing item into tracking with its stock; the form code of the permit was not run in a browser (G-228).

**لماذا هنا:** Single unit with pack conversion, kits that deduct components, serial numbers only. High accounting risk: quantities, cost per unit and valued movements all become unit-aware, and Daftra behaviour was seen only as settings.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Valued movements and StockLedger::reconcile stay exact in base units
- Historical movements keep their quantities and costs
- Bundle cost and journals follow the observation

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- StockLedgerTest with unit conversion
- ReceivingPostingTest and SalePostingTest with batches
- Legacy items default to one unit

**رصد دفترة (2026-10-28):** OBS-A read the unit-template form and the negative-stock options; bundle, batch and conversion behaviour need batch C then D.
- يُضاف (النماذج): Unit template form observed (main unit name, small name <= 25, template name, active, larger-unit rows with name, factor, small name, hidden update_all_costs); the three negative-stock options and the available-versus-on-hand validation select; bundle type 1…
- اعتماد جديد (النماذج): Multi-unit, bundle and tracking behaviour needs the three settings enabled in the trial account and reverted (OBS-C), then write scenarios for bundle cost, batch picking and unit conversion (OBS-D); depends on WP27 as before.
- **قواعد مرصودة** (3؛ النص الكامل وأدلتها في [synthesis.md](observations/obs-a/synthesis.md)): `FRM-R50` إلى `FRM-R52`
- **الدفعة C (2026-10-31، [ملاحظات C](observations/c/NOTES.md)، بحسب تقرير الراصد: لا ملف دليل محفوظ):** enabling units, bundles and tracking added a unit template, a barcode per unit, a tracking type and a 'bundled product' form; the permits of a tracked product were observed; a multi-unit product, creating a bundle and picking a batch are not (`G-149`).

### WP43: Purchase received-state and draft (decision-gated)

**لماذا هنا:** Daftra books an unreceived purchase to 1210 and moves it to inventory in place; the owner declined it (E27) because it conflicts with E15 (reverse and repost). Do it last and only on the owner's word; behaviour is already evidenced (S-13).

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- In-place Daftra behaviour is emulated by reverse and repost with a new revision, never by updating a posted journal (E15)
- Unreceived purchases move no stock; stock and journal move together on confirm
- Draft re-issue takes a new number

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- ReceivingPostingTest: unreceive, confirm, draft, reissue
- StockLedger::reconcile through each state; closed periods; legacy receivings default to received

**رصد دفترة (2026-10-28):** No change: the received-state defaults are recorded, E27 stands.
- **قواعد مرصودة** (1؛ النص الكامل وأدلتها في [synthesis.md](observations/obs-a/synthesis.md)): `FRM-R30`

**الحالة (2026-11-08): done (WP44), partly** — merged into `int/w7` under E47 ([E133](../decisions.md), [report](wp44-report.md), branch head `bc2d8e4`), on dev data only: one export layer that runs the report again on the server with its permission and branch scope and writes CSV, Excel and PDF from the same rows (no new library and no `zip` extension), CSV files for the reports that had none, twelve cards of the books in the reports hub with a route-walk test, thirteen tables that stack as cards on a phone (a Chromium pass on static pages), and paging of the drafts and quotes lists. Left: `WP13-LIST-TOTALS`, `WP7-BALANCES-OVER-TIME`, `G41-A4`, `G-124`, `G-91` (owner), `P8-PRINT` and the PDF settings window (G-233 and G-234); `npm run smoke` and `axe` were not run (G-228).

**لماذا هنا:** Usability slice with no Daftra access needed: server-side PDF and Excel export that re-runs the report with branch scope (the client-posted table export would bypass it), a hub entry, stacked cards on phones and the leftover review findings. Lowest priority, schedulable in spare capacity while credentials are missing.

- يُضاف (تقارير الحسابات): يتحدد التصدير: نمط `<مسار التقرير>.{csv/xlsx/pdf}?<المرشحات نفسها>` يعيد تشغيل التقرير على الخادم، ونافذة إعدادات PDF (اتجاه عمودي/أفقي وحجم خط 10 إلى 50 افتراضي 14) للميزان والأستاذ، و«PDF بدون رسم بياني» للربح والخسارة، ولا تصدير لمراكز التكلفة. التسمية…
- يُضاف (تقارير المبيعات والعملاء): يُحدَّد نطاق التصدير من المرصود: CSV وExcel وPDF وPDF بدون رسم بياني (لتقارير الرسوم) وطباعة، يعيد تشغيل التقرير بنطاق فرع الموظف ويطابق الجدول المعروض بخاتمته. وتُدوَّن عيوب دفترة الأربعة لتُمنع في اختباراتنا: وسم HTML داخل خلايا CSV، وملف `.xls` قديم باسم…
- يُضاف (تقارير المشتريات والمخزون والخزائن): التصدير بالخادم لتقارير المشتريات والمخزون والمصروفات بصيغ CSV (UTF-8 مع BOM) وExcel وPDF مع خيار PDF بلا رسم بياني لتقارير بعينها وزر طباعة، ويعيد تشغيل التقرير بنطاق الفرع نفسه. كشف الخزينة وسجل النشاطات بلا تصدير عند دفترة (فعندنا تصديرهما زيادة اختيارية)…
- يُضاف (الأدوار): لا حزمة تملك الإعدادات العامة، فتُقترح هنا أو في حزمة جديدة: (1) إضافة الرياض والكويت وبغداد وقطر والبحرين وعمّان إلى `get_timezones` (إصلاح صغير فوري)؛ (2) صيغ التاريخ الـ22 بتقويم أم القرى عبر intl؛ (3) أول يوم في الأسبوع وتنسيق القيم السالبة وطريقة عرض…
- يُضاف (الطباعة وإقفال السنة): التصدير من الخادم يشمل CSV وExcel وPDF بالمرشحات نفسها، وإعدادات PDF اتجاه الصفحة (طولي/عرضي) وحجم الخط، وصفوف CSV بعدد حقول الترويسة نفسه (شذوذ كشف المورد)، وعرض التقرير بلا أي كتابة (دفترة ترسل POST تلقائيًا من صفحة التقرير).
- **قواعد مرصودة** (11؛ النص الكامل وأدلتها في [synthesis.md](observations/obs-a/synthesis.md)): `RA-EXPORT-01`، `RSC-UI-02`، `RSC-UI-04`، `RP-XP-01`، `RP-XP-02`، `RP-FMT-01`، `RP-SET-01`، `RP-SET-02`، `RP-SET-04`، `PY-R14`، `PY-R16`

### WP46: E-invoicing (ZATCA phase 2)

**الحالة (2026-11-13): done (WP46), partly** — merged into `int/w8` under E47 ([E139](../decisions.md), [report](wp46-report.md), branch head `0216bee`), on dev data only: a candidate e-invoice document layer, **not an integration**: identification of the parties (eleven codes and a national address on the customer), a document built from a posted sale and tied to its journal, stored once with an ICV counter and a PIH chain and never changed, a UBL 2.1 generator with golden tests, a hash and a QR code with the phase-two tags, an ECDSA signature with a key outside the application tree and an empty transport that logs "not submitted"; **the setting is off, `acc_einvoicing_manage` is sensitive and granted to no one, nothing is sent to any authority and no compliance is claimed.** One migration (four new tables). 326 `public function test` lines added (none removed) and 174 mutations (`wp46-guards.json`). **Not closed:** the check with the official tools (G-239), refusing the edit of a sale with an issued document (G-240), the unsupported scenarios (G-241), CSID, clearance, PDF/A-3, the submission report and counters per unit or branch (G-242).

**لماذا هنا:** Only reserved columns exist. Requirements must be fixed from the official specification when the phase starts, not from Daftra.

- Issued invoices are corrected by notes, never edited (D7)
- Signing keys never stored in the repository

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- Document-level field tests; signing and submission against the official sandbox

**رصد دفترة (2026-10-28):** OBS-A read the e-invoicing party master data and the submission report as a screen only.

- اعتماد جديد (الطباعة وإقفال السنة): باني TLV الخاص بـQR المرحلة الأولى في WP40 يُعاد استعماله في المرحلة الثانية بإضافة الوسوم 6 إلى 9 (التجزئة والتوقيع والمفتاح) من المواصفة الرسمية؛ فيُصمَّم صنفًا نقيًا مستقلًا عن المستند. لا يتغير أن متطلبات المرحلة الثانية تؤخذ من المواصفة لا من دفترة.
- **قواعد مرصودة** (3؛ النص الكامل وأدلتها في [synthesis.md](observations/obs-a/synthesis.md)): `RSC-ZAT-01`، `FRM-R26`، `RP-SET-05`

### WP51: Inter-branch settlement follow-ups (decision-gated)

**لماذا هنا:** WP7 settles a whole pair, refuses a pair whose two sides disagree, and leaves seven questions to the owner (E56 (a) to (g)): partial settlement, an adjustment entry for an unmatched pair, attributing unattributed journals, a record for future transfers, a special permission for cross-branch payments, who is granted `acc_interbranch_settle`, and the settlement-day and inactive-branch rules. None is built until the owner answers; each is a small extension of `InterBranchSettlements`, `InterBranchBalances` and `InterBranchLegs`. Internal design with no Daftra equivalent to read (G-139 in WP21).

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- Same as WP7: each journal balanced inside its own branch, 12044 and 2214 net to zero for the company, branch-number lock order, E43 and the treasury scopes on both sides, a closed period in either branch rejects the whole document
- An existing journal is never rewritten to attribute it: an attribution or an adjustment is a new journal with a reason and an audit line, and the pair still agrees afterwards
- A partial settlement leaves the remainder visible as a balance of the pair and refuses a day before a standing settlement of the same pair (the rule of WP7 section 2.7)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- InterBranchSettlementsTest: the partial amounts that leave the pair in agreement, a pair that does not agree settled by an adjustment, concurrent opposite settlements
- InterBranchBalancesTest: an attributed journal moves from unattributed to its pair; the company difference stays zero
- A branch deactivated with a balance: the chosen rule (forbid or allow) with both outcomes tested

### WP52: Wave-3 owner-gated follow-ups (decision-gated)

**لماذا هنا:** The six packages of wave 3 left questions to the owner whose answers need code (E68 to E73): whether the readiness command gets a `--strict` flag and counts a blank bank name as incomplete; whether the tax account that already carries journals may be re-routed and whether a purchase of a filed period may be deleted; the journal order of a return with a refund payment and whether a suspended return keeps its original sale; a separate number and a separate document for a supplier refund; the typed date of a purchase without a supplier that is paid at save, a branch-restricted reader of the balances and aged-ledger screens, and a supplier opening balance dated in the future; and what a correction of a receiving does with the books off and with a negative location balance. None is built until the owner answers; each is a small change to code that exists. Internal decisions: the Daftra points behind them are `G-141` to `G-145`.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- No change before the owner's answer; the assumption recorded in the report stays the behaviour until then
- A posted journal is never rewritten: any change is a reversal and a new revision (E15), and the three-way tie of customers and suppliers (balance, aging, account) holds after every operation
- Branch scope as in E24, E31 and E45; accounting disabled means no behaviour change (D1)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- For each chosen option, the test that fails without it and the test of the option that was not chosen staying refused or allowed as decided
- The existing tests of the package that holds the behaviour stay green (`ReceivingTest`, `SalePostingTest`, `TaxReportTest`, `AccountingPayablesControllerTest`, `CustomerBalanceReportTest`)

### WP53: Wave-4 owner-gated follow-ups (decision-gated)

**لماذا هنا:** The six packages of wave 4 left questions to the owner whose answers need code or a recorded decision (E81 to E92): the year-end policy (Y-DEC) and its extensions, the closing of a currency with a large denomination, the opening-stock and customer-opening decisions, the draft and quote decisions, the advance-payment model of E74 against the one built, the supplier credit order, the numbering of a refund, the cheque account codes and the sensitivity of `acc_cheques_manage`, supplier cash payments in the cash-up expectation, and the approval to widen a core column. None is changed before the owner answers; the assumption recorded in each report stays the behaviour until then. The letters in E92 give each question.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- No change before the owner's answer; the assumption recorded in the report stays the behaviour until then
- A posted journal is never rewritten: any change is a reversal and a new revision (E15), and the three-way tie of customers and suppliers (balance, aging, account) holds after every operation
- Branch scope as in E24, E31 and E45; accounting disabled means no behaviour change (D1)
- The sensitive permissions stay granted to no one by any migration; a real database is never closed, corrected or opened without the owner's separate permission (E47)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- For each chosen option, the test that fails without it and the test of the option that was not chosen staying refused or allowed as decided
- The existing tests of the package that holds the behaviour stay green (`YearEndClosingTest`, `OpeningStockTest`, `CustomerOpeningTest`, `SalePostingTest`, `CreditSettlementTest`, `CreditRefundsTest`, `ChequeServiceTest`, `CashFlowTest`)

### WP54: Wave-5 owner-gated follow-ups (decision-gated)

**لماذا هنا:** The six packages of wave 5 left questions to the owner whose answers need code or a recorded decision (E93 to E109): whether the retroactive posting may ever run on a production database and who holds its permission, the default branch of legacy expenses and cash-ups and the cost rule of purchase returns and transfers, an automatic liability account per new tax and the approval of the accounts `4204` and `4208`, the sensitivity of `acc_debit_notes_manage` and `acc_bank_reconcile`, the narrowest or the widest scope when roles combine and the meaning of 'own', the scope of own records over vouchers, cheques and reports, whether the credit limit looks at drafts and at a branch, the excluded categories as an exemption or a ban, and a permission of its own for the charges that give money. None is changed before the owner answers; the assumption recorded in each report stays the behaviour until then. The groups in E109 give each question.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- No change before the owner's answer; the assumption recorded in the report stays the behaviour until then
- A posted journal is never rewritten: any change is a reversal and a new revision (E15), and the three-way tie of customers and suppliers (balance, aging, account) holds after every operation
- Branch scope as in E24, E31 and E45; accounting disabled means no behaviour change (D1)
- The sensitive permissions stay granted to no one by any migration; a retroactive batch is never approved on a real database without the owner's separate permission, and a backup comes first (E47, E93)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- For each chosen option, the test that fails without it and the test of the option that was not chosen staying refused or allowed as decided
- The existing tests of the package that holds the behaviour stay green (`BackfillServiceTest`, `TaxDefinitionsTest`, `SalePostingTest`, `BankReconciliationTest`, `RecordScopeTest`, `RoleServiceTest`, `SalesDebitNotesTest`, `PriceControlsTest`, `CreditLimitTest`, `SensitivePermissionsTest`)

### WP55: Wave-6 owner-gated follow-ups (decision-gated)

**لماذا هنا:** The six packages of wave 6 left questions to the owner whose answers need code or a recorded decision (E110 to E122): one account for the exchange gains and losses as Daftra or the two defaults `4209` and `5410`, whether and when multi-currency may be switched on in a production database and who holds `acc_currencies_manage`, a stock account per item (which needs the three stock ties rebuilt on account and location), the date of the stocktake journal and the sensitivity of the two approval permissions, the negative-stock rule of the permits, the supplier and customer on a permit, the rule that splits paid, unpaid and returned and the definition of profit in the sales reports, whether the operational reports and the other reports narrow by the own-records scope alike, the revaluation at period end and three decimals on documents, business events in the activity log, whether sales orders are switched on and when the recurring generator runs from cron. None is changed before the owner answers; the assumption recorded in each report stays the behaviour until then. The groups in E122 give each question.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- No change before the owner's answer; the assumption recorded in the report stays the behaviour until then
- A posted journal is never rewritten and never repriced: any change is a reversal and a new revision (E15, E66 (1)), and the three-way tie of customers and suppliers (balance, aging, account) holds after every operation
- Multi-currency stays off in production until the owner decides: the journals in the base currency stay byte-identical (the golden `T-06` to `T-19`) and the sensitive permission `acc_currencies_manage` is granted to no one by any migration (E47, E112)
- Branch scope as in E24, E31 and E45; accounting disabled means no behaviour change (D1); no mail leaves the system and the recurring generator is run from the owner's cron, never installed by the code (E47, E117)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- For each chosen option, the test that fails without it and the test of the option that was not chosen staying refused or allowed as decided
- The existing tests of the package that holds the behaviour stay green (`SalePostingBuilderTest`, `SalePostingTest`, `ReceivingPostingTest`, `TreasuryTransfersTest`, `ItemRoutingAccountsTest`, `StocktakeTest`, `StockPermitTest`, `RevenueReportTest`, `ProfitReportTest`, `SensitivePermissionsTest`)

### WP56: Wave-7 owner-gated follow-ups (decision-gated)

**لماذا هنا:** The six packages of wave 7 left questions to the owner whose answers need code or a recorded decision (E123 to E135): several categories per customer, the lighter required-field rules and whether suspended differs from inactive; editing the distribution of a posted manual journal, the mandatory centre on automatic journals, the 200% cap and the automatic sub-account of a shipping company; switching the purchase cycle on and who is granted its permissions, whether 'approved' is required, several conversions per document and custom statuses; the template designer, the Arabic amounts in words, the QR image and whether it is mandatory, the format of tag 5 and the sensitivity of `acc_custom_fields_manage`; four decimals on the item price, the pre-assembled bundle, an expired batch sold by permission and switching units and tracking on in a database that holds stock; and the export limits, the PDF font size and the account codes in Excel. None is changed before the owner answers; the assumption recorded in each report stays the behaviour until then. The groups in E135 give each question.

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- No change before the owner's answer; the assumption recorded in the report stays the behaviour until then
- A posted journal is never rewritten and never repriced: any change is a reversal and a new revision (E15, E66 (1)); the distribution of a posted manual journal stays reverse and repost until the owner decides (E51, E124)
- Units, bundles and tracking stay off in production until the owner decides, and tracking is never switched off or on while a tracked item holds a balance that its serials and batches do not explain (E132); the purchase cycle stays off and its permissions granted to no one (E128)
- Branch scope as in E24, E31 and E45; accounting disabled means no behaviour change (D1); no mail leaves the system (E47)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- For each chosen option, the test that fails without it and the test of the option that was not chosen staying refused or allowed as decided
- The existing tests of the package that holds the behaviour stay green (`CostCenterServiceTest`, `JournalServiceTest`, `PurchaseDocumentsTest`, `PurchaseOrderReceiptsTest`, `StockTrackingTest`, `UnitLineTest`, `DocumentQrTest`, `AmountInWordsTest`, `ReportExportTest`, `StockLocationLifecycleTest`, `SensitivePermissionsTest`)

### WP57: Wave-8 owner-gated follow-ups (decision-gated)

**لماذا هنا:** The six packages of wave 8 left questions to the owner whose answers need code or a recorded decision (E136 to E145): the permissions a role may never carry (ten now) and what happens to the roles that hold them on deployment, the line that takes the foreign-currency rounding difference and whether to repost, the tie rows refused by name or converted, the picker threshold and the nine report screens, the performance thresholds and the direction of two default accounts; the e-invoice rules (refusing the edit of a sale with an issued document, the chain per company, unit or branch, which unsupported scenario first, who is granted `acc_einvoicing_manage`, the official samples); the default asset accounts, credit acquisition and credit sale, revaluation, the declining formula, scheduling the preview, posting the open branch, hiding a sold asset, who is granted `acc_assets_depreciate`, the balance check of an asset entered as in the books and delegating a category; the agent (separating who triggers from who approves, the control accounts, the thresholds, switching it on, the schedule, who is granted its permissions); the portal (switching it on and its scope, an account per contact, who holds `portal_manage`, HTTPS and a rate limit, the print template); and the integration settings (the secrets screens, keys limited to a branch, a stopped payment method kept by a recurring expense, any gateway or channel).

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- No change before the owner's answer; the assumption recorded in the report stays the behaviour until then
- A posted journal is never rewritten and never repriced: a foreign-currency invoice already posted is not reposted unless the owner says so (E15, E136); a depreciation run is previewed and approved by a person and never posted unattended (E140)
- Nothing is sent to any authority, mail address or provider: the e-invoice layer sends nothing, the agent uses no model, the portal sends no mail and the notification rules have no outgoing channel (E47); `acc_einvoicing_manage`, `acc_assets_depreciate`, `accounting_ai`, `acc_ai_approve`, `portal_manage` and `api_keys_manage` stay sensitive and granted to no one until the owner decides
- A role never carries a permission that posts, moves money or changes the settings until the owner changes the list (E136, E144); the portal, the agent, the e-invoice layer and the API stay off
- Branch scope as in E24, E31 and E45; accounting disabled means no behaviour change (D1)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- For each chosen option, the test that fails without it and the test of the option that was not chosen staying refused or allowed as decided
- The existing tests of the package that holds the behaviour stay green (`RoleGrantsTest`, `SalePostingBuilderTest`, `TieCheckTest`, `AccountPickerTest`, `EInvoiceServiceTest`, `AssetRegisterTest`, `DepreciationRunTest`, `ProposalServiceTest`, `PortalGuardTest`, `PaymentMethodsTest`, `ApiKeysTest`, `SensitivePermissionsTest`)

### WP58: Wave-9 owner-gated follow-ups (decision-gated)

**لماذا هنا:** The six packages of wave 9 left questions to the owner whose answers need code or a recorded decision (E146 to E154): the sales debit note and the credit limit, revising a credit opening balance whose credit was refunded, running the E57 preview on a copy before deployment and accepting a closer that rolls back; the foreign-currency rules (refusing, skipping or applying a base-currency credit on a foreign document with an exchange difference entry, refusing or converting the profit report and the revenue and net-position reports, a note or a refusal for the legacy reports, the cash refund of a foreign credit); the clock (which clock is the reference and who aligns the host, whether the application stamps `sales_payments.payment_time`, the four unknown zone names, FAIL or REVIEW for a payment stamped with a day that has not come, scheduling the depreciation preview and the agent, and which way for a database that holds history); access (who is opened and who is closed by the module rule, the answer 400 for an array on the other settings screens, the my-records notice, the wider my-records scope of the statements); the cashier (the refusal of cancelling a sale with a return or one operation, the basis of the minimum price, the rounding of the old tax summary, the name of a suspended invoice, the kits with price option 2); and the pickers and measurements (the deleted customers in the filter, the permission of the ledger search, an item picker or a notice, bounding or paging the heavy report families, the thresholds, the browser passes at 768px and 1440px).

**ثوابت لا تُكسر:**
- No change before the owner's answer; the assumption recorded in the report stays the behaviour until then
- A posted journal is never rewritten and never repriced (E15); a refunded credit is never taken back, spent or refunded twice until its refund is cancelled (E146); a base-currency credit is never applied to a foreign document silently (E147)
- A database that holds history never has its server zone changed alone: the times are converted with CONVERT_TZ on a rehearsal copy first and the accountant checks that the day of every document and entry and every closed period did not move (E148)
- A module opens only for the holder of its own grant, and a permission never starts with the id of another module (E119, E149); roles and sensitive permissions stay as E144 left them
- Branch scope as in E24, E31 and E45; accounting disabled means no behaviour change (D1)

**اختبارات الإثبات:**
- For each chosen option, the test that fails without it and the test of the option that was not chosen staying refused or allowed as decided
- The existing tests of the package that holds the behaviour stay green (`CreditRefundsTest`, `CreditSettlementTest`, `SupplierSettlementTest`, `CreditLimitTest`, `ProfitReportTest`, `ReadinessRunnerTest`, `ClockChecksTest`, `EmployeeTest`, `EscapingAssertionsTest`, `SalesControllerTest`, `PriceControlsTest`, `AccountPickerTest`, `BuiltAssetsTest`)

## 4. أُسقط لأنه منفَّذ فعلًا (بدليله في الكود)

- G-01 (hybrid entity sub-accounts): EntityAccountService.php covers customer, supplier, stock_location and treasury; Customer.php:235 calls sync on save; fill_accounts command exists
- G-03 (separate cost journal): entry_type revenue/cogs in the unique key; SalePosting::postCost posts it in the sale transaction
- G-07 and G-10 (input tax under liabilities, Daftra default chart): DefaultChart.php adopts the Daftra chart incl. 215001/215002; DefaultChartTest
- G-11 and ACC-25 (accounting on/off switch): AccountingSettings.php accounting_enabled; intentional difference D1
- G-15 (edit and delete model): decided by E15 (reverse and repost); only the edit screen remains, tracked as G-37
- G-16, G-24, G-47, G-58 (cost and valuation precision): CostPrecision and ReceivingCostPrecision migrations widen to six decimals, T-11 and T-14 exact
- G-22 (cancel of issued invoice): SalePosting.php:164 throws issued_document_locked; E21; correction path is G-30
- G-29 and SAL-40 (delete customer payment, unwind of spent credit): intentional differences E15 and E25, T-10 case stays 'differs' by decision
- G-33 and G-54 (company-wide balances screens): intentional E24 and E31
- G-39 (purchase fixed discount per unit): E32 done, FIXED_LINE marks legacy lines (Constants.php:152); the whole-invoice part is G-59
- G-50 (supplier payment deletion unwind): matches Daftra (T-19, E30)
- G-60 (credit timing of a debit note): intentional E34, T-17 stays 'differs' by decision
- G-44 (opening stock for pre-existing inventory): OpeningStock.php implements plan, post, reverse; only production activation remains (E26-ACTIVATION)
- G-62 (treasury deposit and withdraw scopes): TreasuryScopes.php enforced on every money path; residuals are the roles scope (G-09) and the interpretation check (S35-D4)
- G-65 (treasury selection at the register): Sales.php:1597-1615 registerTreasury, carried through suspended sales and posting
- G-61 and G-41 transfer parts (money and stock between branches): TreasuryTransfers.php, migrations 20261102/20261103, InterBranchPosition.php; residuals kept as G-61 (pairwise), P6-ASSUMPTION-7, INTERBRANCH-SETTLEMENT and G-41 (permit document) (superseded 2026-10-28: G-61 (pairwise), P6-ASSUMPTION-7 and INTERBRANCH-SETTLEMENT were done in WP7; the new residuals are G-134 to G-139 and G-41 (permit document))
- CUS-07 (per-customer sub-account and statement): EntityAccountService, CustomerLedger, AccountingReceivables::getCustomer; the matrix cell is stale
- ACC-32 (editable automatic journal number): intentionally not copied (E16); no number-edit method in JournalService.php
- ACC-33 (reverse a journal): JournalService::reverse() links to the original, required reason and date
- PUR-11 (auto-settlement from supplier credit): SupplierSettlement.php; E29, E30, E34 are owner-approved differences; only the settle-button observation remains (G-49)
- R-01 and R-07 (Daftra reads of the chart and of the branches and cheques modules): executed; R-06 (routing option lists): read in rtP2 (ev_8c1f06a3...), the scenario table still says pending
- Matrix rows already 'exact and tested': SAL-01, SAL-02, SAL-03, SAL-06, SAL-28, SAL-30, SAL-41, INV-13, INV-14, INV-18, PUR-02, ACC-01, ACC-02, ACC-03, ACC-04, ACC-06, ACC-07, ACC-08, ACC-10, ACC-11, ACC-13, ACC-15

## 5. بنود خطرة على البيانات التاريخية وصورتها الآمنة

Items risky for historical data, with the safe dev-only form of each. (1) E33 / G-59 (whole-purchase discount) and its sales twin SAL-07: the E32 incident shows that reinterpreting a stored column silently changes old documents (LegacyFixedPurchaseDiscount had to mark them). Safe form: additive nullable header columns defaulting to 0 or null; a golden test that every existing fixture (T-11 to T-19, T-06 to T-10 for sales) produces byte-identical journals when the discount is empty; no recalculation, re-post or backfill of existing receivings or invoices; issued sales stay locked (E21); owner approval of E33 first (superseded on 2026-10-28: E33 was implemented on development data under E47 in WP15 in exactly this safe form, with nullable columns defaulting to null or 0, a golden file of 22 purchase scenarios and no recalculation of any existing receiving; SAL-07 was done the same way in WP16); development data only, production only by separate owner permission. (2) G-57 (explicit re-post of opening stock at current item cost): re-pricing the opening journal changes the base of the weighted average and breaks StockLedger::reconcile for later movements and COGS. Safe form: per-location explicit option, default off, with a reason and a before/after plan; refused when any later movement exists at the location or the period is closed; old revision stays reversed and visible; never a batch or catalogue-wide reprice; development data only, production only by separate owner permission (E47). Also historical-risk and handled the same way: E38-SCAN (the legacy fixed-discount migration rewrites discount_type and its down() changes meaning, so scan first, back up, rollback by restore only); A6 (retroactive posting: preview journals excluded from all balances, dry run only on dev data); CUS-03 and E26-NEG-LINES / E26-LIMITS (opening balances: reverse-and-repost only, triple tie, provisional); YE-01 (closing entries: preview, reversal, never on production without permission); E26-ACTIVATION, E40-BANK-DETAILS, E41-STAMP (owner-run operations on real databases); G-27, G-31, G-08 (new nullable date and number columns whose defaults reproduce current aging and numbering; existing documents are never renumbered or redated); G-37 and G-36 (edit or unreceive of posted receivings must go through reverse-and-repost and must not reprice consumed cost); G-28 (journal-order swap applies to new journals only, historical journal numbers are not rewritten); G-12 and G-63 (base-currency journals unchanged, rate 1). (2) E58 / WP17 (edit or delete of a saved receiving line recomputes the average): safe form: reverse the valued movement at its recorded value and repost under a new revision, never rewrite a posted journal or a posted cost of goods sold; refuse when it would drive a location negative or change a cost already consumed by posted sales; a golden file that receivings never edited stay byte-identical; WP17 must also redistribute the whole-invoice discount (E54) inside the same transaction. (3) E57 / WP23 (credit application order): the change is one branch of CreditSettlement::apply and moves credit between invoices of existing customers on the next settlement run, so it is deployed with the release that carries the T-10 reclassification and a read-only report of the invoices whose applications would move. (4) A6 / WP12 (done on dev data on 2026-11-01, E93, in exactly this safe form): previews excluded from every balance and report, an approval that must equal the preview and is refused as a whole if anything changed, cancel removes the previews, approval off by a setting and a sensitive permission granted to no one, opening balances and the start date required, and a batch is never approved on a production database without the owner's separate permission, after a backup. The retroactive posting skips the description template (G-168). Also (5) the debit note of WP34 rebuilds `customer_credit_applications` a second time after CustomerOpenings: back up before that migration.

## 6. ملاحظات المُركِّب

Method and verification. Source: five analyst lists merged by meaning; all aliases are kept in the item's aliases field and repeated as '[aliases: ...]' at the start of the summary. 168 backlog items are listed, each exactly once, plus the dropped list; 50 packages. Items I re-verified against code (grep/read): G-28 journal order (SalePosting.php:81-86), G-57 (OpeningStock.php:201,207), G-48 (no 'branch' anywhere in Models/Reports, Controllers/Reports.php, report_helper.php), G-45/INV-15 (StockLedger/StockPosition referenced only inside app/Libraries/Accounting), P1-PICKER (AccountService::all() at AccountingJournals.php:40 and AccountingChart.php:91,203,245,270), expense and cash-up posting absent (no Accounting reference in Expense/Expenses/Cashup/Cashups), due_date/payment_term/purchase_tax/credit_limit/price_list/recurring/sales_order/invoice_discount absent, income statement/balance sheet/cash flow/year_end absent, backfill = schema only, cheques = payment-method label only, per-item and main-per-entity routing refused (RoutingType.php:28-32), customer opening balance blocked (OpeningBalanceService CONTROL_KEYS), no refund path in SupplierAdvances, no journal-metadata edit in JournalService, no description template, no tests using totals_decimals, no sale-level oversell test. Two NEW findings not in any analyst list: P6-SENSITIVE-PERMS (docs call acc_treasuries_manage sensitive but AccountingGuard::SENSITIVE omits it and acc_payables_manage, so Employees.php:180 lets non-admins grant them) and A2A-STOCK-ACCESS (Items screens and StockAdjustment ignore the employee's branch scope; only Sales and Receivings enforce it). Both are in WP1. Conflicts resolved: G-62, G-61, G-41, G-65, G-44 were 'done' in one analyst list and 'partial' in another: the done parts are dropped and the genuine residuals kept under their own ids (G-61 pairwise, P6-ASSUMPTION-7, INTERBRANCH-SETTLEMENT, G-41 permit document, G-09 roles scope, S35-D4). 'A7' is used in the docs both for the vouchers-and-cheques phase (alias of G-64) and for the pairwise inter-branch balance in g61-report.md (alias of G-61); G-45 versus INV-15 disagreed on whether aging and turnover layouts were observed: valuation was (repK, 1,720), aging and turnover layouts are treated as unobserved and left to REP-14; SAL-05 was 'partial' in code but 'blocked' for proof, kept as blocked because only S-03 can settle it; matrix text is stale for ACC-28 (15 acc_* permissions exist), CUS-07, FIN-01, ACC-05, PUR-01 (CHK-F). Ordering: WP1-WP19 need no Daftra access and are executable now (correctness, then integrity and auditability, then commercial-flow soundness); WP20-WP29 are the Daftra observation batches and the Daftra-dependent correctness builds (credentials are absent, E47); WP30-WP43 are architecture-fit and parity work; WP44-WP50 are lowest priority (polish, performance, deferred domains, umbrellas). WP44 and WP45 need no Daftra access and can be scheduled in spare capacity while credentials are missing even though they rank last. Owner-gated items: E33 is no longer waiting for approval (implemented on development data under E47 in WP15; production still needs separate owner permission), the cash-up treatment needs an owner decision (A4), the unreceived-purchase state was declined by E27 (WP43), and any production activation, merge to master, deploy or production data change needs separate permission (E47).

Production is unaffected only when the server and the application share a clock: a deployment note.

the WP36 merge 24 code and test files (among them `Sale.php`, `Sale_lib.php`, `Sales.php`, `AccountingReceivables.php`, `AccountingSetup.php`, `AccountingGuard.php`, `MigrationChecks.php` and `SensitivePermissionsTest.php`) and `Accounting.php`, `Accounting_receivables.php`, `Accounting_settings.php`, `Module.php` and `Sales.php` in 46 languages; the WP30 merge `Sales.php`, `Sale.php`, `CustomerDocuments.php`, `AccountingGuard.php`, `SensitivePermissionsTest.php`, `SalesControllerTest.php`, `SaleTest.php`, `LanguageFilesTest.php` and `Accounting.php` and `Module.php` in 46 languages; the WP29 merge `AccountingTreasuries.php`, `AccountingGuard.php`, `AccountingError.php`, `MigrationChecks.php`, the treasury form, `TreasuryServiceTest.php`, `EmployeesControllerTest.php` and `Accounting.php` and `Accounting_treasuries.php` in 46 languages. **The combined semantics are in E108:** `SalePostingBuilder::invoice` reading `SaleAmounts::figures` (shipping and adjustment lines of WP34, the tax lines per tax account of WP25 with the rest of the tax, shipping tax included, going to `tax.output`) and `discountLine($sale, ...)` reading the included tax of each line, with `forDatabase` building the same builder for the live and for the retroactive posting and no longer passing the global `tax_included`; `Sale::save_value` with two new named nullable parameters after `returnOverrideReason` (`headerCharges` of WP34, `priceListId` of WP36) and `get_info` with an optional viewer (WP30); eighteen sensitive permissions (fourteen before, plus `acc_backfill_manage`, `acc_roles_manage`, `sales_price_override` and `sales_credit_override`) and twenty-six `acc_*` permissions (twenty-two before), where `acc_cheques_manage`, `acc_debit_notes_manage` and `acc_bank_reconcile` are the ones that are not sensitive and that a role may carry; eight migrations in order (`20271500000000_RetroactiveBackfill` additive and exempt, `20271600000000_PerTaxInclusive` HEAVY, `20271700000000_BankReconciliation` HEAVY, `20271800000000_RolesAndRecordScope` and `20271800000001_RolesPermissions` additive, `20271900000000_InvoiceHeaderCharges` DATA, `20271900000001_SalesDebitNotes` HEAVY, `20272000000000_PricingAndCreditControls` HEAVY), every one classified or exempt by a program that applies the rule of `MigrationChecksTest` to the 94 files; `JournalService::DOCUMENT_SOURCES` with fourteen sources (`sales_debit_note` added) and `CREDIT_SOURCES` with four; 21 readiness checks (`A6-BACKFILL`). **Conflicts and corrections while integrating:** the reports of WP29, WP30 and WP34 call some matrix rows partial, which is not in the key (read as 'differs', as `CUS-03` was; the counts 20, 132, 60 and 19 of 231, taken by counting the rows); the WP29 report asked for three new rows and the matrix already has `FIN-14` for the statement import (it moves, and two rows are added: `FIN-19` and `FIN-20`); the WP30 report asked for `ACC-28` or the nearest row and the nearest are `SET-13`, `SET-15` and `INV-09`; the WP29 report says twenty-three `acc_*` and fourteen sensitive and the WP12 report calls its permission the fifteenth sensitive one, while the merged code has twenty-six and eighteen; the WP30 report says 71 uses of `closeThrough('2026-10-31')` in the tests and the tree has 18 (G-180); the WP36 report says the minimum price compares the price without the tax and the code compares the typed price with no tax logic (G-193); E77 observed that Daftra checks drafts by the credit limit and WP36 does not (a deliberate difference of its report); the WP34 report counts 186 and 108 new tests and the diff has 296 (and the WP29 report counts 203 in new files against 228 in the whole package); the reports wrote `E-؟؟` for the decision number and `G-WP34-...` for gap ids, now E93 to E109 and G-166 onwards; the three reports of WP25, WP30 and WP34 asked for a matrix status partial and a paragraph 'counts are revised at merge', which this integration did. **Test counts:** from the reports: WP12 263, WP25 154, WP29 203 in new files, WP30 267 (115 in nine new files), WP34 186 and 108, WP36 252 and 35; guards 215, 101, 221, 167, 183 and 135. **Counted by the integrator in the branch diffs** (`public function test` lines added, from `60dd925`): 263, 154, 228, 267, 296 and 286, which is 1,494 with 5 removed or renamed (1,489 net), and 1,022 guards in the six `wp*-guards.json` files (215, 101, 221, 167, 183, 135). **What was not re-run:** the guard tools (the counts are the reports' and the JSON files'), the full suite, the readiness command and every command that uses the databases, because the full suite of the merged branch was running on them when this was written. Reports of this wave are drafts the owner can veto under E47.

Found by reading the merged code (in no report): `EscapingAssertionsTest` fails on the merged branch with one method of W95 (`SalesControllerTest::testGetSuspended_ShowsTheNumberOfASuspendedDocumentAsTextAndNeverAsMarkup`, line 6712 reads the parsed `->getBody()`), written by the code integrator; the readiness has 31 checks in twelve groups; `RaceWorkerTest.php` lives under `tests/Support`, which the three parts of the wave-8 run did not cover, so it is run by name. **Two things no package owns:** (a) thirteen to twenty-three existing tests fail between 00:00 and 05:00 UTC because the application of the tests is on America/New_York while `payment_time` takes the clock of the database (UTC): this is `G-180` (`W91-CLOCK-TESTS`), and W93 found it is also a latent production defect (`G-261`: `Sale` does not write `sales_payments.payment_time`; the owner decision is open); (b) the CI Coding Standards check (php-cs-fixer) is red at the head (431 of 4,814 files as the integrator reported) and is planned for the serial closeout after the integration (`G-275`). The full suite of the merged branch was running while this was written: no test, no disabling tool, no command that touches a database, no `npm` and no `php-cs-fixer` was run here, so each point above found by reading is proven by a test before it is relied on. The merger count of `public function test` lines added in the branch diffs from 0679560 is 91, 101, 120, 60, 56 and 51 (479 in all, 7 removed), and the guard files hold 48, 85, 122, 43, 40 and 62 mutations (400).
